Taken together, our results rule out superpolynomial quantum speedups for computing static properties, but provide an avenue for quantum advantage in simulating impurity physics out of equilibrium.

</details>


### [163] [Robust exponential lower bounds for fermionic and bosonic Gaussian ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02172)
*Fuchuan Wei,Kong-Wing Wu,Zhengwei Liu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色与费米系统中高斯秩（Gaussian rank）的下界问题，证明任意纯非高斯态的张量幂的近似边界高斯秩随模数至少指数增长，误差低于1时均成立，从而为基于分解的经典模拟设下根本限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟的威力与极限是理解量子计算优势的核心。主流模拟范式基于将目标态相干分解为经典可处理的自由态（如稳定子态、高斯态），其分解秩决定模拟复杂度。证明该秩的强下界（如qubit stabilizer rank）极为困难，本文旨在建立高斯情形下的鲁棒指数下界。

Method: 证明结合了多种技术：对费米子，将问题归约到四模并利用Majorana谱界；对玻色子，采用高斯后选择配合基于熵的秩界。研究对象涵盖玻色与费米系统，费米情形要求态具有确定宇称。

Result: 证明了对有限模上任意纯非高斯态（费米情形需确定宇称），其张量幂的近似边界高斯秩在任意固定范数误差低于1时至少指数增长。作为具体例子，得到四模费米GHZ态与玻色单光子态的显式指数下界；玻色结果不需对目标态能量作任何假设。

Conclusion: 非高斯性普遍导致指数级高斯分解复杂度，为玻色和费米系统的基于分解的经典模拟设定了根本性限制。

Abstract: The power and limitations of classical simulation are central to understanding quantum computational advantages. A leading simulation paradigm is based on coherent decomposition into classically tractable free states where the decomposition rank determines the simulation complexity. Proving strong lower bounds on this number is a notoriously difficult and mathematically rich problem, as exemplified by the qubit stabilizer rank problem. Here, we study the Gaussian version of this problem in both bosonic and fermionic systems and establish robust exponential lower bounds on Gaussian rank. In particular, we prove that for every pure non-Gaussian state on finitely many modes, with definite parity in the fermionic case, the approximate border Gaussian rank of its tensor powers grows at least exponentially at any fixed norm error below one. Our proofs combine reduction to four modes with Majorana spectral bounds for fermions, and Gaussian postselection with an entropy-based rank bound for bosons. As concrete examples, we derive explicit exponential lower bounds for the four-mode fermionic GHZ state and the bosonic single-photon state. The bosonic results require no assumption on the energy of the target state. Our results show that non-Gaussianity universally entails exponential Gaussian decomposition complexity, setting fundamental limitations on decomposition-based classical simulation of bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [164] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文利用全置换不变性约束信道相关性带来的增益：优化相干信息随块长的每用值收敛到单次最大值，并在有限块长处达到上确界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信道间的跨使用相关性可提升量子通信速率，导致量子容量的确定涉及任意大块长的优化这一核心困难；作者希望量化置换不变性对这种增益的限制。

Method: 对任意有限维无记忆信道施加全置换不变性（permutation invariance），结合De Finetti约化与置换协变性构造共享变分框架，分析优化后的每用相干信息、纯源纠缠生成以及输出熵。

Result: 优化的每用相干信息收敛到单次使用最大值，并在有限块长处达到其supremum；有限块超可加性仍可能，但渐近优化约化为单次信道使用。对置换不变输入，纯源纠缠生成满足强逆定理：速率高于单次相干信息最大值时保真度趋于零。还揭示了完全有界范数与夹层Rényi信道量在抗替代者判别与纠缠辅助通信中的共同结构，并给出多重性与可加性结果的系统性替代证明；同时证明置换不变输入下每用输出熵不能低于单次最小值，该熵限对Rényi阶大于1及冯诺依曼熵均成立。

Conclusion: 全置换不变性将渐近优化约化为单次信道使用，把对称性的作用从通信界扩展到输出熵控制，并统一了若干信道度量与容量的可加性/多重性结论。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [165] [Learning SYK Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02178)
*Anurag Anshu,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sitan Chen,Yeongwoo Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文研究从Gibbs态副本中学习稠密SYK哈密顿量的问题，突破现有方法依赖几何局域性或有限相互作用度的限制，利用模型的随机平均场结构，证明在任意常数温度下可用多项式样本以逆多项式精度学习整个哈密顿量，并在足够小逆温度下构造拟多项式时间学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习算法通常依赖几何局域性或有限相互作用度，而SYK模型中每个四次相互作用与Θ(n³)个其他项重叠，导致这些方法不适用。因此需要一个能处理稠密、高重叠相互作用的SYK哈密顿量学习新方法。

Method: 利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构，克服稠密交互的障碍。对SYK耦合进行随机性分析，在任意常数温度下证明可以高概率使用多项式数量样本学习整个哈密顿量至逆多项式精度；在逆温度为足够小常数时，构造拟多项式时间学习算法，该算法在性质上不同于样本高效算法。

Result: 在任意常数温度下，以高概率（对SYK耦合）证明可逆多项式精度、多项式样本量地学习整个哈密顿量。当逆温度限制为足够小常数时，构造了拟多项式时间学习算法，与样本高效算法在性质上不同。

Conclusion: 通过利用SYK的随机平均场结构可克服稠密相互作用学习障碍，实现样本高效且达到逆多项式精度的哈密顿量学习，并进一步给出了拟多项式时间算法，扩展了哈密顿量学习的适用模型范围。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the dense Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian from copies of its Gibbs state. Existing algorithms for Hamiltonian learning typically rely on geometric locality or bounded interaction degree and therefore do not apply to SYK, where each quartic interaction overlaps with $Θ(n^3)$ others. We show that this obstruction can be overcome by exploiting the random mean-field structure of the model. At any constant temperature, we prove that with high probability over the SYK couplings, the entire Hamiltonian can be learned to inverse-polynomial accuracy using polynomially many samples. Furthermore, when the inverse temperature is restricted to be a sufficiently small constant, we construct a quasipolynomial-time learning algorithm which is qualitatively different from the sample-efficient algorithm.

</details>


### [166] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究被全局酉变换遮蔽的哈密顿量学习问题，证明其DQC1-hard，并提出SPICES算法高效恢复谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料建模中，学习方法（如平均场理论、格林函数、密度泛函理论）常需处理非相互作用模式构成的哈密顿量，但其非相互作用结构可能被未知全局酉变换$U$遮蔽。目标是直接恢复能量权重$E_i$而无需重构$U$，这一设定在实际材料模拟中具有重要动机。

Method: 论文形式化了一个哈密顿量$H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$的学习任务，其中$I$为非相互作用模式集合，$O$为不相交的旁观模式集合，$U$未知。首先证明该问题DQC1-hard。随后提出SPICES（single-particle inference via contour estimators），结合Hadamard测试与新颖的经典后处理程序，利用单粒子能量与复逆温度下配分函数零点的傅里叶频率之间的对应关系，高效恢复谱分布。

Result: 证明问题DQC1-hard；SPICES在若干自然假设下能以Wasserstein距离高效恢复谱分布。该方法利用了单个准粒子的单粒子能量控制复逆温度下配分函数零点的傅里叶频率这一对应关系。

Conclusion: 准粒子谱在后续材料计算中广泛出现，SPICES简单且易于执行，有望成为材料模拟中量子应用的有前景途径。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>

### [167] [A Compact, High-Data-Rate, High-Sensitivity GMOT-Based Light-Pulse Atom Interferometer toward Time-Multiplexed, Multi-Axis Inertial Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01735)
*Benjamin Smith,Adrian Orozco,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于光栅磁光阱（GMOT）的紧凑型光脉冲原子干涉仪（LPAI），可在高达100 Hz的高数据率下同时实现高加速度灵敏度（20 Hz时550 ng/√Hz）和良好零偏稳定性（71 ng），适用于动态环境下的现场量子惯性/重力传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光脉冲原子干涉仪在量子惯性与重力传感中具有应用前景，但在动态环境中，振动引入的噪声以及自由落体原子与光脉冲之间空间重叠失配会导致性能退化，难以在不牺牲灵敏度的前提下实现稳健运行。

Method: 采用紧凑、坚固的GMOT架构：基于真空中微加工光栅芯片与单束激光冷却光束，实现亚多普勒冷却的高数据率冷原子制备，并以此构建GMOT基LPAI系统。

Result: 系统在高数据率（最高100 Hz）下，于20 Hz、45 Hz、70 Hz分别实现550 ng/√Hz、1.49 µg/√Hz、6.31 µg/√Hz的加速度灵敏度，零偏稳定性达71 ng。

Conclusion: 该紧凑坚固架构为高动态环境下的现场可部署量子惯性/重力传感器以及时分复用多轴惯性传感提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Light-pulse atom interferometry has emerged as a promising approach for developing quantum inertial and gravity sensors for real-world applications, but achieving robust operation in dynamic environments without sacrificing sensitivity remains challenging. To mitigate performance degradations resulting from vibration-induced noise and mismatches in the spatial overlap between free-falling atoms and light pulses, we demonstrate a first-of-a-kind high-data-rate, high-sensitivity grating magneto-optical trap (GMOT)-based light-pulse atom interferometer (LPAI). This system uses a compact, rugged GMOT architecture, based on an in-vacuum microfabricated grating chip and a single laser-cooling beam, to enable high-data-rate GMOT-based cold-atom generation with sub-Doppler cooling. Our GMOT-based LPAI operates at high data rates (up to 100 Hz), while simultaneously achieving both high acceleration sensitivity (550 ng/rt-Hz at 20 Hz, 1.49 ug/rt-Hz at 45 Hz, and 6.31 ug/rt-Hz at 70 Hz) and good bias stability (71 ng). This compact, rugged architecture paves the way for field-deployable quantum inertial and gravity sensors in high dynamics and for time-multiplexed, multi-axis inertial sensing.

</details>


### [168] [Stark effect of hydrogenic ions as a test of the indefinite-metric formalism of the eight-component relativistic wave equation for spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02060)
*Paulus C. Tjiang,Sylvia H. Sutanto,Vincentius E. W. Tjia*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统计算了氢型离子（H到U^91+）的n=1–3能级在Schrödinger、Dirac和FV½(Feshbach–Villars线性化)理论下的Stark位移（一阶与二阶），用以检验FV½不定度规微扰论能否精确重现Dirac结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FV½波函数能重现Dirac氢原子光谱且解空间加倍、具有不定内积，其精确Stark位移理论上应等于Dirac结果，但其不定度规微扰论能否给出正确结果尚不明确。

Method: 用Dalgarno–Lewis方法计算全部谱求和（含连续谱），得到Schrödinger、Dirac与FV½三种理论的一阶和二阶Stark位移；并采用精确的壳内/正则二阶位移分解、第二矩求和规则等技术进行分析。

Result: FV½与Dirac位移逐能级一致：一阶达到工作精度（闭式根），二阶达到45位有效数字。两者缺一不可：缺少显式自旋–场耦合则一阶根变为复数；缺少负范数态则(Zα)²系数错误。范数符号与宇称在加倍空间中相互锁定。相对论线性Stark效应小有限倍，但第二矩求和规则表明非相对论线性Stark强度守恒，缺失部分以奇异(Zα)⁻²二阶项重现；对带精细结构伙伴的能级给出(Zα)⁰系数解析式，并将(Zα)²系数的π²项与奇异项关联。氢n=2的Dirac弱/强场交叉点约在2.8 kV cm⁻¹，微扰窗口随Z⁵增长。

Conclusion: FV½不定度规微扰论在完备处理自旋–场耦合与负范数态的前提下能精确重现Dirac的Stark位移，验证了其等价性。

Abstract: The eight-component relativistic wave equation for spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ particles of Robson and Staudte (FV$\frac{1}{2}$), a Feshbach--Villars linearization of the Feynman--Gell-Mann equation, reproduces the Dirac hydrogenic spectrum with a doubled solution space and an indefinite inner product. Its exact Stark shifts must equal the Dirac ones; whether its indefinite-metric perturbation theory delivers them is not guaranteed. We compute the first- and second-order Stark shifts of the $n=1$--3 levels of hydrogenic ions from H to U$^{91+}$ in the Schrödinger, Dirac and FV$\frac{1}{2}$ theories, evaluating all spectral sums, continuum included, by the Dalgarno--Lewis method. The FV$\frac{1}{2}$ and Dirac shifts coincide level by level, to working precision at first order (closed-form roots) and to 45 significant figures at second order. Both ingredients are needed: without the explicit spin--field coupling the first-order roots become complex, and without the negative-norm states the $(Zα)^2$ coefficient is wrong. Norm sign and parity are locked in the doubled space: a potential lifting the $2s_{1/2}$--$2p_{1/2}$ degeneracy needs its own spin--gradient term, and shifts placed by hand on the $κ$-labeled states give a spurious imaginary linear Stark shift. The relativistic linear Stark effect is smaller by finite factors, but a second-moment sum rule shows the non-relativistic linear Stark strength is conserved, the missing part reappearing in singular $(Zα)^{-2}$ second-order terms. For levels with a fine-structure partner, an exact intra-shell/regular split of the second-order shift gives the $(Zα)^0$ coefficients analytically and relates the $π^2$ terms of the $(Zα)^2$ coefficients to the singular ones. The Dirac weak/strong-field crossover for $n=2$ of hydrogen lies near 2.8~kV cm$^{-1}$, and the perturbative window set by the fine structure grows as $Z^5$.

</details>
